## Known Issues

K1 The registry export marked FINAL is a snapshot taken before void processing (governance Table G2).

K2 A primary node log was truncated during the stage; see the TRUNCATION incident in alerts.mbox.

K3 Spreadsheets from the Mac workstation use the 1904 date system; their _control sheet says so.

K4 Some alerting relays deliver a message twice; Message-ID identifies an alert.
